# Video Encoding

## Data Types

The related data types and data structures are defined as follows:

-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_CHN\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099114): Defines the maximum number of channels.
-   [OT\_VENC\_TEXTURE\_THRESHOLD\_SIZE](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408259038): Defines the number of texture-level rate control thresholds.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_TILE\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258538): Defines the maximum number of supported tiles.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_SSE\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698105): Defines the number of SSE regions.
-   [OT\_VENC\_VEDU\_IP\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697753): Defines the number of encoding logic units on the chip.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_FRAME\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658229): Defines the maximum number of frames in the multi-frame image sending interface.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_MOSAIC\_RECT\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408259094): Defines the maximum number of mosaic regions in the multi-frame image sending interface.
-   [OT\_VENC\_QP\_HIST\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698309): Defines the QP histogram array size.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_SVC\_RECT\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698361): Defines the number of intelligent detection target rectangles.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MD\_THRESHOLD\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099150): Defines the number of MD detection thresholds.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MD\_LEVEL\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258582): Defines the number of MD detection level values.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_PACK\_INFO\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258910): Defines the maximum number of additional data items contained in a stream packet.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_MPF\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697957): Defines the maximum number of MPF images.
-   [OT\_VENC\_PRORES\_MAX\_ID\_CHAR\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098970): Defines the maximum number of characters in the PRORES vendor name.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_HIERARCHY\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698349): Defines the maximum number of hierarchical encoding layers.
-   [OT\_VENC\_SCALING\_LIST\_SIZE\_16](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658481): Defines the scaling list size.
-   [OT\_VENC\_SCALING\_LIST\_SIZE\_64](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099190): Defines the scaling list size.
-   [OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_QT\_COEF\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658485): Defines the JPEG quantization table size.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MJPEG\_QT\_COEF\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658005): Defines the MJPEG quantization table size.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_ROI\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658321): Defines the maximum number of H.264/H.265 ROIs.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_JPEG\_ROI\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258686): Defines the maximum number of JPEG ROIs.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MAX\_BITRATE](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658309): Defines the maximum bitrate.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MIN\_BITRATE](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658513): Defines the minimum bitrate.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MJPEG\_MAX\_BITRATE](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408259062): Defines the MJPEG maximum bitrate.
-   [OT\_VENC\_MJPEG\_MIN\_BITRATE](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099210): Defines the MJPEG minimum bitrate.
-   [ot\_venc\_h264\_nalu\_type](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698313): Defines the H.264 stream NALU type.
-   [ot\_venc\_ref\_type](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408259110): Defines the frame type and reference attributes of the H.264 skip-frame reference stream.
-   [ot\_venc\_jpege\_pack\_type](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408259070): Defines the JPEG stream PACK type.
-   [ot\_venc\_h265\_nalu\_type](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697845): Defines the H.265 stream NALU type.
-   [ot\_venc\_prores\_pack\_type](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698149): Defines the PRORES stream PACK type.
-   [ot\_venc\_data\_type](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697901): Defines the stream type union.
-   [ot\_venc\_pack\_info](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098986): Defines the structure of other stream packet data contained in the current stream packet.
-   [ot\_venc\_pack](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099182): Defines the frame stream packet structure.
-   [ot\_venc\_h264\_stream\_info](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698253): Defines H.264 protocol stream characteristic information.
-   [ot\_venc\_jpeg\_stream\_info](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658505): Defines JPEG/MJPEG protocol stream characteristic information.
-   [ot\_venc\_h265\_stream\_info](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258986): Defines H.265 protocol stream characteristic information.

-   [ot\_venc\_h265\_adv\_stream\_info](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698157): Defines H.265 protocol advanced stream characteristic information.
-   [ot\_venc\_stream](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099186): Defines the frame stream type structure.
-   [ot\_venc\_stream\_buf\_info](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099090): Defines the stream buffer information structure.
-   [ot\_venc\_h265\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408259090): Defines the H.265 encoder attribute structure.
-   [ot\_venc\_h264\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698239): Defines the H.264 encoder attribute structure.

-   [ot\_venc\_jpeg\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408259134): Defines the JPEG/MJPEG encoder attribute structure.
-   [ot\_venc\_prores\_frame\_rate](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098694): Defines the PRORES protocol frame rate.

## OT\_VENC\_MAX\_CHN\_NUM<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099114"></a>

**Description**

Defines the maximum number of channels. Default values differ for different solutions.

**Definition**

SS528V100/SS524V100

```
#define  OT_VENC_MAX_CHN_NUM	256
```

SS625V100/SS522V101/SS626V100: 

```
#define  OT_VENC_MAX_CHN_NUM	128
```

Hi3403V100: 

```
#define  OT_VENC_MAX_CHN_NUM	64
```

**Notes**

Maximum number of channels: Default values differ for different solutions. Since the maximum number of channels involves memory allocation, users can redefine the maximum number of channels as needed. The specific method is: for Linux, set the module parameter g\_venc\_max\_chn\_num when loading ssxx\_venc.ko to specify the actual maximum number of channels, which takes priority over OT\_VENC\_MAX\_CHN\_NUM.

Usage method as follows:

SS528V100/SS524V100

```
insmod ot_venc.ko    g_venc_max_chn_num=128
```

**Related Data Types and Interfaces**

None.

## OT\_VENC\_MAX\_BITRATE

**Description**

Defines the maximum bitrate, in Kbps.

**Definition**

SS528V100/SS625V100/SS522V101: 

```
#define  OT_VENC_MAX_BITRATE	(60 * 1024)
```

SS524V100/SS626V100: 

```
#define  OT_VENC_MAX_BITRATE	(200 * 1024)
```

Hi3403V100: 

```
#define  OT_VENC_MAX_BITRATE	(160 * 1024)
```

**Notes**

The maximum bitrate of SS524V100/SS626V100 is for reference only, subject to the actual achievable maximum bitrate.

**Related Data Types and Interfaces**

None.

## OT\_VENC\_MIN\_BITRATE

**Description**

Defines the minimum bitrate, in Kbps.

**Definition**

```
#define OT_VENC_MIN_BITRATE 2
```

**Notes**

None.

**Related Data Types and Interfaces**

None.

## OT\_VENC\_MJPEG\_MAX\_BITRATE

**Description**

Defines the MJPEG maximum bitrate, in Kbps.

**Definition**

SS528V100/SS625V100/SS522V101: 

```
#define  OT_VENC_MJPEG_MAX_BITRATE	(60 * 1024)
```

SS524V100/SS626V100: 

```
#define  OT_VENC_MJPEG_MAX_BITRATE	(200 * 1024)
```

Hi3403V100: 

```
#define  OT_VENC_MJPEG_MAX_BITRATE	(1024 * 1024)
```

**Notes**

Higher bitrates on Hi3403V100 may cause performance degradation. When using high bitrates, testing is required to verify whether performance requirements are met.

**Related Data Types and Interfaces**

None.

<tr id="row19323mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="66%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.1 "><p id="p19325mcpsimp"><a name="p19325mcpsimp"></a><a name="p19325mcpsimp"></a>OT_VENC_JPEG_PACK_DCF</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="34%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.2 "><p id="p19327mcpsimp"><a name="p19327mcpsimp"></a><a name="p19327mcpsimp"></a>DCF type.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row19328mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="66%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.1 "><p id="p19330mcpsimp"><a name="p19330mcpsimp"></a><a name="p19330mcpsimp"></a>OT_VENC_JPEG_PACK_DCF_PIC</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="34%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.2 "><p id="p19332mcpsimp"><a name="p19332mcpsimp"></a><a name="p19332mcpsimp"></a>DCF ECS type.</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

**Notes**

-   OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_PACK\_DCF and OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_PACK\_DCF\_PIC are only enabled when the JPEG attribute mode is configured as multi-channel receive mode. The OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_PACK\_ECS packet in single-channel receive mode is the sum of OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_PACK\_DCF and OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_PACK\_DCF\_PIC data packets in multi-channel receive mode.
-   Multiple ECS (entropy-coded segments) form a complete frame. When ECS low latency needs to be enabled, ecs\_output\_en must be enabled. In this case, an interrupt is reported after each ECS is encoded. The channel attribute is\_by\_frame must be FALSE, meaning the stream is received by packet.

**Related Data Types and Interfaces**

None.

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="51%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.2 "><p id="p15469181955219"><a name="p15469181955219"></a><a name="p15469181955219"></a>离MotionObjectrelativelynearSmearingAndResidualRegionLCU(HEVC)Count. </p>

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="51%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.2 "><p id="p10111239523"><a name="p10111239523"></a><a name="p10111239523"></a>离MotionObjectrelativelyfarSmearingAndResidualRegionLCU(HEVC)Count. </p>
</td>

</tbody>
</table>

**Notes**

-   move\_scene32x32\_numandmove\_scene\_bitsStatisticsEnabledNeedthis interfacess\_mpi\_venc\_set\_fg\_protectEnableForegroundmacroBlockLevelBitrateControl. 
-   sseStatisticsvalueHasError. Calculatessse\_sum, sse\_valwhen, NotStatisticsEachoneLCU\*LCUBlockbottompart4Line, rightedge5ColumnPixel; 
-   psnrCalculatesas follows:

    lcu\_hor = \(H265E\_LCU\_SIZE - 5\);

    lcu\_ver = \(H265E\_LCU\_SIZE - 4\);

    dl\_data = \(td\_double\)\(\(\(td\_u64\)255 \* 255 \* lcu\_hor \* lcu\_ver \* mse\_lcu\_cnt + 64\) \>\> 7\) / sse\_sum;

    psnr = 10.0 \* log10\(dl\_data\);

-   The `sse_sum` and `sse_val` values obtained by the user are calculated as (S × lcu_hor × lcu_ver / lcu_size / lcu_size + 64) / 128, where S is the raw statistics value. 
-   sseStatisticsvalueHasError. 
-   Whensse\_sum as 0when, Returnspsnrvalue as 99.00. 

**Related Data Types and Interfaces**

-   ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_stream
-   ss\_mpi\_venc\_set\_fg\_protect

</td>

</tbody>
</table>

**Notes**

-   SS528V100, SS625V100Not supportedframe\_buf\_ratioAdjust, Mustset to 100. 
-   `frame_buf_ratio` sets the ratio of the saved frame buffer size relative to the raw size. For example, setting it to 80 means the frame buffer is 80% of the raw size. Setting `frame_buf_ratio` to 0 (i.e., 100%) may affect the following functions, and setting it too small may result in lower encoding bitrate: 
    1.  PFrameI sliceMayexitAdjustmentFrameRefreshIBlockAndIn advanceEndI slice, InvolvesInterface: ss\_mpi\_venc\_set\_intra\_refresh; 
    2.  Breathing EffectShouldCertainFrameonMayInvalidation, InvolvesInterface: ss\_mpi\_venc\_set\_debreath\_effect; 
    3.  QPMAPmeterCannotstrongmakeSpecifiedskipBlock, SkipWeightmeterCannotstrongmakeSpecifiedskipBlock, strongmakeSpecifiedskipBlockMayCausesSystemAbnormal, DriverInternalnotdoChecks, Userself-LineEnsure; 
    4.  ROIBackgroundlowFramerateencodingMayexitPartialFrameNon-ROIRegionCannotencoding as p\_skipBlock, InvolvesInterface: ss\_mpi\_venc\_set\_roi\_bg\_frame\_rate; 
    5.  CertainScenarioSpecified as p\_skipFrameMayInvalidation, Such asencodingInstantaneousBitrateExceedsThresholdwhenloseFrameStrategy, InvolvesInterface: ss\_mpi\_venc\_set\_frame\_lost\_strategy; 
    6.  skip tendencyMayInvalidation, InvolvesInterface: ss\_mpi\_venc\_set\_skip\_bias

**Related Data Types and Interfaces**

None.

```
    ot_payload_type type;
```

**Notes**

-   SetsourceFrameratesrc\_frame\_ratewhen: 

    -   VENCFramerateControl as increaseFrameMode, Thensrc\_frame\_rateShouldset to VENCInputFramerate. 
    -   VENCFramerateControl as SubtractFrameModeOrnotPerformsFramerateControl, src\_frame\_rateShouldTheset to GenerateTimeRefActualFramerate, RCbased onsrc\_frame\_rateStatisticsActualFramerateAndPerformsBitrateControl. 
        -   IfencodingImagesourceIsVIwhen, Thensrc\_frame\_rateset to VIActualOutputFramerate, Because as TimeRefIsVIOutputwhenGenerate; 
        -   IfVIandVPSSOnlinewhen, Thensrc\_frame\_rateset to VPSSActualOutputFramerate, Because as TimeRefIsVPSSOutputwhenGenerate; 
        -   IfVIandVPSSOfflinewhen, Due toVPSSOnlyGroupFramerateControl, VPSSGroupsourceFramerateSetShouldTheandencodingsrc\_frame\_rateSetsame, src\_frame\_rateset to VIActualOutputFramerate, Because as TimeRefIsVIOutputwhenGenerate. 

    falseSetVIOutputFramerateIs30: 

    -   IfVENCnotPerformsFramerateControl, src\_frame\_rateShouldTheset to 30. 
    -   IfVENCPerformsFramerateControl, VENCFramerateControlIf as increaseFrameMode, dst\_frame\_rateShouldTheSetGreater Than30, VENCFramerateControlIf as SubtractFrameMode, dst\_frame\_rateShouldTheSetLess Than30. 
    -   IfVENCFramerateControl as SubtractFrameMode, SimultaneouslyRCPerformsFramerateControl, thenmostfinalFrameratewillBythe two values relativelysmallvalue as standard. Such asVENCset to sourceFramerate30Frame, TargetFramerate6Frame, RCset to sourceFramerate30, TargetFramerate as 10Frame, thenmostfinalTargetFramerate as 6Frame. 

-   SetTargetFrameratedst\_frame\_ratewhen, Height16bitUsed forIndicatesnumerator, low16bitIndicateschild. 
    -   IfUserSetTargetFramerate as number, Setdenominator high16bit as 0, i.e., CanIndicatesnumber. For exampleSetsrc\_frame\_rateEqual to25, dst\_frame\_rateEqual to12, ThenIndicateswillFrom25FrameInputImageGetexit12FramePerformsencoding, Itsremaining13Framewilllosedrop. 
    -   If setViFrmRateEqual to25, dst\_frame\_rateEqual to15/2, ThenIndicatesRequirementencoding2-second periodencoding15Frame. 

-   dst\_frame\_rateSetMethodas follows: Ifneedset to numberFramerate25, ThenCanAdoptingThe followingMethoddst\_frame\_rate= 25; Ifneedset to Fractional frame rate15/2, ThenAdoptingThe followingMethod: dst\_frame\_rate= 15+(2<<16). 
-   WhenUsesVENCandRCtwoLevelFramerateControlwhen, VENCTargetFramerateLess ThanRCTargetFramerate, Such asVENCset to sourceFramerate30Frame, TargetFramerate6Frame, RCset to sourceFramerate30, TargetFramerate as 10Frame, thiskindSituationMayindexstartBitrateControlnotAccurate; RecommendedUserSetVENCTargetFramerateGreater ThanEqual toRCTargetFramerate, ByThistoachievetoUserTargetFramerate. 
-   WhenVPSSonlyBindencodingAndUsesAUTOin mode, UsesVENCFramerateControlCansaveSaveVPSScan. 
-   If setBitrateExceedsChipManualspecified maximum real-time bitrate, ThenCannotEnsureReal-timeencoding.

## ot\_venc\_h265\_qvbr\_param<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658421"></a>

**Description**

DefinesH.265ProtocolEncoding ChannelQVBRRate Control modeAdvanced ParametersConfigure . 

**Definition**

```
typedef struct {
    td_u32  max_i_proportion;
    td_u32  min_i_proportion;
    td_s32  max_reencode_times;
    td_bool qpmap_en;
    ot_venc_rc_qpmap_mode qpmap_mode;
    td_u32  max_qp;
    td_u32  min_qp;
    td_u32  max_i_qp;
    td_u32  min_i_qp;
    td_s32  max_bit_percent;
    td_s32  min_bit_percent;
    td_s32  max_psnr_fluctuate;
    td_s32  min_psnr_fluctuate;
```

```
} ot_venc_h265_qvbr_param;
```

**Notes**

-   Scenarioconfigured as OT\_VENC\_SCENE\_1 when,  as balance image quality, cb\_qp\_offset, cr\_qp\_offsetDefaultconfigured as -3. 
-   Scenarioconfigured as OT\_VENC\_SCENE\_0, OT\_VENC\_SCENE\_2when, cb\_qp\_offset, cr\_qp\_offsetDefault valuenotsameSolutionvaries, where Hi3403V100Default value is -3, Othervalue as 0. 
-   scaling\_list\_en as 0when as NoneQuantization Table. 
-   scaling\_list\_en as 1, scaling\_list\_tu4\_valid/scaling\_list\_tu8\_valid/ scaling\_list\_tu16\_valid/ scaling\_list\_tu32\_valid as 0when,  as ProtocolDefaultQuantization Table. 
-   scaling\_list\_en/scaling\_list\_tu4\_valid/scaling\_list\_tu8\_valid/ scaling\_list\_tu16\_valid/ scaling\_list\_tu32\_valid as 1when,  as Userself-DefinesQuantization Table. 
-   scaling\_list\_tu4\_valid/scaling\_list\_tu8\_valid/ scaling\_list\_tu16\_valid/ scaling\_list\_tu32\_validOnly supportedSimultaneously as 0Or1. 
-   SS528V100/SS625V100/SS524V100/SS522V101/Hi3403V100/SS626V100Only supportedNoneQuantization Table.

qpmap\_value\_i, qpmap\_value\_pget valueMeaning: 

-   Whenvalue as 0,1,2,…,31: CorrespondingdeltaQP:0,1,2,…,31

skipmap\_valueget valueMeaning:

-   Height4bitRepresentsleftLCUSkipweightvalue; 
-   low4bitRepresentsrightLCUSkipweightvalue;

**Definition**

```
typedef struct {
    td_u32 idx;
    td_bool enable;
    td_u32 fg_level;
```
